##### stage-3-class.puml
@startuml
' ======================================================================
' SC2002 Restaurant Rush - Stage 3 class diagram (source of truth)
' Colour key: green = new in Stage 3, yellow = changed since Stage 2, blue = unchanged
' Render: see docs/diagrams/src/README.md
' ======================================================================
!pragma layout smetana
title Restaurant Rush: Stage 3 class diagram

skinparam shadowing false
skinparam classAttributeIconSize 0
skinparam defaultFontName SansSerif
skinparam defaultFontSize 12
skinparam nodesep 28
skinparam ranksep 38
skinparam linetype polyline
skinparam ArrowColor #333333
skinparam noteBackgroundColor #F6F7F2
skinparam noteBorderColor #8C8C80
skinparam noteFontSize 11
skinparam legendBackgroundColor #EDEDED
skinparam legendBorderColor #8C8C80
skinparam class {
  BorderColor #3F6B96
  BackgroundColor #DCEBF7
}
skinparam stereotypeCBackgroundColor transparent
hide empty members
hide circle

' ---------- boundary and control ----------
class RestaurantCLI <<boundary>> #FAE8A6 {
  -controller: GameController
  -input: Scanner
  -out: PrintStream
  +start(): void
  +readWaiterChoice(): String
  +readChefChoice(): String
  +readHostChoice(): String
  +readHappyHourChoice(): boolean
  +displayTurnStatus(Restaurant): void
  +displayMessage(String): void
  +displayError(String): void
  +displayResult(boolean, Restaurant): void
}

class GameController <<control>> #FAE8A6 {
  -restaurant: Restaurant
  -schedule: ArrivalSchedule
  -currentTurn: int
  -maxTurns: int
  -cli: RestaurantCLI
  +startGame(): void
  +getCurrentTurn(): int
  +getMaxTurns(): int
  -executeTurn(): void
  -processHappyHourActivation(): void
  -applyHappyHourRecovery(): void
  -processScheduledArrival(): void
  -coordinateStaffActions(): void
  -coordinateHostAction(): void
  -coordinatePaymentProcessing(): void
  -processEndOfTurnUpdates(): void
  -evaluateGameResult(): boolean
}

class ArrivalSchedule {
  -arrivalTurns: int[]
  -nextCustomerId: int
  -customerTypes: List<IntFunction<Customer>>
  +isArrivalTurn(turn: int): boolean
  +createArrival(turn: int): Customer
}

' ---------- restaurant and policies ----------
class Restaurant #FAE8A6 {
  -tables: List<Table>
  -customers: List<Customer>
  -waitingQueue: Queue<Customer>
  -menu: Menu
  -happyHour: HappyHour
  -waiter: Waiter
  -chef: Chef
  -cashier: Cashier
  -host: Host
  -revenueCents: int
  +registerArrival(Customer): void
  +findAvailableTable(): Table
  +findOldestEligiblePayer(turn: int): Customer
  +getMenu(): Menu
  +getHappyHour(): HappyHour
  +getWaiter(): Waiter
  +getChef(): Chef
  +getCashier(): Cashier
  +getHost(): Host
  +getCustomers(): List<Customer>
  +getWaitingQueue(): Queue<Customer>
  +recordPayment(amountCents: int): void
  +releaseTableOf(Customer): void
  +getPaidCustomerCount(): int
  +getServedCustomerCount(): int
  +getAverageSatisfaction(): double
  +getRevenueCents(): int
  +handleAbandonment(Customer): void
  +getUnhappyDepartureCount(): int
}

class HappyHour #CCEBD2 {
  {static} -DISCOUNT_PERCENT: int = 20
  {static} -RECOVERY: int = 15
  {static} -DURATION: int = 2
  -used: boolean
  -remainingTurns: int
  +activate(): boolean
  +isActive(): boolean
  +isAvailable(): boolean
  +getRemainingTurns(): int
  +applyDiscount(cents: int): int
  +getRecoveryAmount(): int
  +endTurn(): void
}

class Menu {
  -items: List<MenuItem>
  +getItems(): List<MenuItem>
  +getItem(index: int): MenuItem
  +size(): int
  +getMostExpensive(): MenuItem
}

class MenuItem #FAE8A6 {
  -name: String
  -priceCents: int
  -preparationUnits: int
  +MenuItem(name: String, priceCents: int, preparationUnits: int)
  +getName(): String
  +getPriceCents(): int
  +getPreparationUnits(): int
}

class ComboMeal #CCEBD2 {
  -first: MenuItem
  -second: MenuItem
  +ComboMeal(first: MenuItem, second: MenuItem)
  +getComponents(): List<MenuItem>
}

' ---------- staff and table ----------
class Waiter #FAE8A6 {
  +seatCustomer(Customer, Table): void
  +takeOrder(Customer, Menu, HappyHour, currentTurn: int): Order
  +serveOrder(Customer, currentTurn: int): void
}

class Host #CCEBD2 {
  +seatCustomer(Customer, Table): void
}

class Chef {
  +prepare(Order): void
}

class Cashier {
  +processPayment(Customer, Restaurant): void
}

class Table {
  -tableId: int
  -occupant: Customer
  +isAvailable(): boolean
  +seat(Customer): void
  +release(): void
  +getOccupied(): Customer
}

' ---------- customers ----------
abstract class Customer #FAE8A6 {
  -customerId: int
  -satisfaction: int
  -status: CustomerStatus
  -order: Order
  -servedTurn: int
  -readyTurn: int
  +getId(): int
  +getStatus(): CustomerStatus
  +getSatisfaction(): int
  {abstract} +getTypeLabel(): String
  +chooseMenuItem(Menu): MenuItem
  +calculateBillCents(basePriceCents: int): int
  {abstract} #getSatisfactionLoss(): int
  #adjustSatisfaction(delta: int): void
  +reduceSatisfaction(): void
  +recoverSatisfaction(amount: int): void
  +isAwaitingService(): boolean
  +onServed(turnsWaited: int): void
  +assignOrder(Order): void
  +getOrder(): Order
  +markSeated(): void
  +markServed(turn: int): void
  +canFinishEating(turn: int): boolean
  +markReadyToPay(turn: int): void
  +canPay(turn: int): boolean
  +markPaid(): void
  +shouldAbandon(): boolean
  +markAbandoned(): void
}

class RegularCustomer {
  +getTypeLabel(): String
  #getSatisfactionLoss(): int
}

class VIPCustomer {
  +getTypeLabel(): String
  #getSatisfactionLoss(): int
  +calculateBillCents(basePriceCents: int): int
}

class CriticCustomer {
  {static} -LATE_SERVICE_TURNS: int = 2
  {static} -LATE_PENALTY: int = 10
  +getTypeLabel(): String
  #getSatisfactionLoss(): int
  +chooseMenuItem(Menu): MenuItem
  +onServed(turnsWaited: int): void
}

class Order {
  -orderId: int
  -item: MenuItem
  -completedUnits: int
  -status: OrderStatus
  -billCents: int
  -turnTaken: int
  +Order(item: MenuItem, billCents: int, turnTaken: int)
  +advancePreparation(): void
  +isReady(): boolean
  +markServed(): void
  +markPaid(): void
  +cancel(): void
  +getStatus(): OrderStatus
  +getBillCents(): int
  +getTurnTaken(): int
}

enum CustomerStatus #EAF1F7 {
  WAITING
  SEATED
  ORDERED
  SERVED
  READY_TO_PAY
  PAID
  ABANDONED
}

enum OrderStatus #EAF1F7 {
  PLACED
  PREPARING
  READY
  SERVED
  PAID
  CANCELLED
}

' ---------- layout skeleton (hidden links keep the rows tidy) ----------
' Smetana cannot route labelled same-rank links, so sideways links carry no mid-link label.
Restaurant -[hidden]right- GameController
GameController -[hidden]right- ArrivalSchedule
Host -[hidden]right- Table
Table -[hidden]right- Waiter
Waiter -[hidden]right- Chef
Chef -[hidden]right- Cashier
Cashier -[hidden]right- Menu
Customer -[hidden]right- Order
Order -[hidden]right- MenuItem
RegularCustomer -[hidden]right- VIPCustomer
VIPCustomer -[hidden]right- CriticCustomer
CriticCustomer -[hidden]right- CustomerStatus

' ---------- control and boundary ----------
RestaurantCLI "cli 1" <-down-> "controller 1" GameController
GameController "1" -right-> "schedule 1" ArrivalSchedule
GameController "1" -left-> "restaurant 1" Restaurant
RestaurantCLI .down.> Restaurant : displays
ArrivalSchedule .down.> Customer : <<create>>\nRegular, VIP, Critic

' ---------- composition (Restaurant owns these) ----------
Restaurant "1" *-down- "1" HappyHour : happyHour
Restaurant "1" *-down- "1" Host : host
Restaurant "1" *-down- "2" Table : tables
Restaurant "1" *-down- "1" Waiter : waiter
Restaurant "1" *-down- "1" Chef : chef
Restaurant "1" *-down- "1" Cashier : cashier
Restaurant "1" *-down- "1" Menu : menu

' ---------- aggregation (customers arrive and leave) ----------
' Multiplicities at the Customer end sit in the labels: three links meet at Customer's corner and end labels would overlap there.
Restaurant "1" o-down- Customer : customers [0..*]
Restaurant "1" o-down- Customer : waitingQueue [0..*]\n{ordered, FIFO}

' ---------- menu ----------
Menu "1" o-down- "3..*" MenuItem : items
MenuItem <|-down- ComboMeal
' Inheritance and aggregation join the same two boxes, so the aggregation is coloured and its multiplicity sits in the label
ComboMeal o-[#B5541C,bold]up- MenuItem : components [2]

' ---------- domain associations ----------
Table "1" -down-> Customer : occupant [0..1]
Customer "1" -right-> Order : order [0..1]
Order "0..*" -right-> "1" MenuItem
Customer -down-> CustomerStatus : status
Order -down-> OrderStatus : status
Customer <|-down- RegularCustomer
Customer <|-down- VIPCustomer
Customer <|-down- CriticCustomer

' ---------- dependencies ----------
GameController .[#777777].> Waiter
GameController .[#777777].> Host
GameController .[#777777].> Chef
GameController .[#777777].> Cashier
GameController .[#777777].> HappyHour
GameController .[#777777].> Customer
Host .right.> Table
Waiter .left.> Table
Host .down.> Customer : seats
Waiter .down.> Customer : seats / serves
Waiter .down.> Order : <<create>>
Waiter .down.> Menu : reads
Waiter .up.> HappyHour : applyDiscount
Chef .down.> Order : advances
Cashier .down.> Customer : charges
Cashier .down.> Order : reads bill
Cashier .up.> Restaurant : recordPayment,\nreleaseTableOf
Customer .up.> Menu : chooses from

' ---------- annotations ----------
note top of Restaurant
  <b>COMPOSITION</b>
  Restaurant creates and owns its Tables, Menu,
  HappyHour and staff; they exist only as part of
  one Restaurant. Customers are aggregated:
  they arrive and leave. The waitingQueue and
  customers links both point at Customer.
end note

note bottom of Order
  <b>ENCAPSULATION</b>
  status is private and changes only through
  advancePreparation(), markServed(), markPaid(),
  cancel(); illegal transitions are rejected.
  Legal: PLACED → PREPARING → READY → SERVED → PAID
  (PLACED → READY directly for a 1-unit dish);
  PLACED / PREPARING / READY → CANCELLED if the
  customer leaves.
  billCents is set once in the constructor with no
  setter: the price is locked when ordered.
end note

note bottom of CustomerStatus
  <b>CUSTOMER LIFECYCLE</b>
  WAITING → SEATED → ORDERED → SERVED →
  READY_TO_PAY → PAID; WAITING / SEATED / ORDERED
  → ABANDONED when satisfaction reaches 0.
  Served, eating and READY_TO_PAY customers
  lose no satisfaction.
end note

note left of Customer
  <b>POLYMORPHISM</b>
  getSatisfactionLoss(), calculateBillCents(),
  chooseMenuItem() and onServed() are overridden.
  GameController and Waiter call them on Customer
  and never check the type.
  Loss per waiting turn: Regular 8, VIP 5, Critic 12.
  <b>ENCAPSULATION</b>
  satisfaction is private; subclasses change it only
  through adjustSatisfaction() (clamped 0..100).
  <b>TURN GUARDS</b>
  canFinishEating(turn): only after the serving turn.
  canPay(turn): only after the READY_TO_PAY turn.
end note

note right of Waiter
  <b>BILLING</b>
  takeOrder(): bill = happyHour.applyDiscount(
    customer.calculateBillCents(price))
  VIP discount first, then Happy Hour; locked in
  the new Order. VIP Burger: 1500, 1350, 1080 cents.
end note

note right of HappyHour
  <b>HAPPY HOUR</b>
  Once per game, at turn start; active for that turn
  and the next. 20% off orders taken; +15 satisfaction
  (max 100) for customers waiting for a table or food.
end note

note right of CriticCustomer
  <b>CRITIC RULE</b>
  Loses 12 per waiting turn. Always orders the most
  expensive menu item (Salad + Pasta, $24.30, 3 units).
  Served more than 2 turns after ordering: loses an
  extra 10 (onServed). A 3-unit combo cannot be served
  that fast, so this Critic always loses the extra 10.
end note

note bottom of ComboMeal
  <b>COMBO</b>
  Hollow-triangle line: a ComboMeal is a MenuItem. Orange
  diamond line: it holds 2 MenuItem components (a MenuItem
  can be a component of 0..* combos).
  Stage 3 menu =Salad, Burger, Pasta + 2 ComboMeals
  (5 entries), registered once at game setup.
  Customer.chooseMenuItem(menu) picks from it.
  price = (a.price + b.price) x 90%, rounded:
  (sumCents x 90 + 50) / 100; units = a.units + b.units.
  Salad + Burger $21.60, 2 units; Salad + Pasta $24.30,
  3 units. Choice rule: customerId % menu.size(), so
  indexes 3 and 4 are the combos.
end note

legend left
  <b>KEY</b>
  <back:#CCEBD2>  green  </back> new in Stage 3
  <back:#FAE8A6>  yellow  </back> changed since Stage 2
  <back:#DCEBF7>  blue  </back> unchanged
  <b>Changed since Stage 2</b> (nothing removed)
  Customer: + recoverSatisfaction(); isAwaitingService()
    is now public
  Waiter: takeOrder(...) also takes HappyHour
  MenuItem: no longer final (ComboMeal extends it)
  Restaurant: + host, happyHour and their getters; the
    menu now holds two ComboMeals
  GameController, RestaurantCLI: Host and Happy Hour steps
end legend
@enduml

##### stage-3-sequence.puml
@startuml
' ======================================================================
' SC2002 Restaurant Rush - Stage 3 sequence diagram (source of truth)
' Scenario: a VIP orders a Burger while the manager has Happy Hour on.
' Turns 1-5 are unrolled so that "later turn" rules are visible:
'   T1 arrive/seat -> T2 order + Chef READY -> T3 serve -> T4 READY_TO_PAY -> T5 pay
' Turn 4 also has the base-setting arrival of customer 2, seated by the Host.
' Render: see docs/diagrams/src/README.md
' ======================================================================
title Restaurant Rush: Stage 3 sequence diagram (page 1 of 3)
mainframe **sd** VipOrdersBurgerDuringHappyHour

skinparam style strictuml
skinparam defaultFontName SansSerif
skinparam defaultFontSize 12
skinparam shadowing false
skinparam ParticipantPadding 6
skinparam BoxPadding 4
skinparam sequenceMessageAlign center
skinparam responseMessageBelowArrow true
skinparam sequenceGroupHeaderFontStyle bold
skinparam sequenceGroupBackgroundColor #EEF3FA
skinparam sequenceGroupBodyBackgroundColor transparent
skinparam sequenceDividerBackgroundColor #E3EAF4
skinparam sequenceDividerBorderColor #7C93B8
skinparam sequenceDividerFontStyle bold
skinparam noteBackgroundColor #FFF4CF
skinparam noteBorderColor #B08A2E
skinparam noteFontSize 11
skinparam sequenceLifeLineBorderColor #999999
skinparam ArrowColor #222222

actor Manager as M order 10
boundary "cli : RestaurantCLI" as cli order 20
control "game : GameController" as game order 30
entity "schedule : ArrivalSchedule" as sched order 40
entity "restaurant : Restaurant" as rest order 50
entity "hh : HappyHour" as hh order 60
entity "t1 : Table" as t1 order 70
entity "host : Host" as host order 85
entity "waiter : Waiter" as waiter order 90
entity "chef : Chef" as chef order 110
entity "cashier : Cashier" as cashier order 120
' vip (order 80) and order (order 100) are declared where they are created

note over M, rest
  <b>Stage 3 | base setting (18 turns, 2 tables)</b>
  <b>Start:</b> turn 1, restaurant empty, 2 free tables. A VIP arrives
  (satisfaction 100). Burger = $15.00, 1 preparation unit.
  <b>Manager choice:</b> turn on Happy Hour at turn 1 or 2 (or never).
  <b>Outcome:</b> the VIP is PAID on turn 5, bill locked at <b>$10.80</b>,
  satisfaction <b>95</b>. If never activated: bill $13.50, satisfaction 90.
  <b>Host:</b> left at WAIT in turns 1-3 (the Waiter seats the VIP); in turn 4
  it seats customer 2, who is not traced further.
end note

M -> cli : start game
cli -> game : startGame()
note right of game : GameController holds only the restaurant. Whenever it needs the\nHappyHour, the Menu or a staff member it takes it from the Restaurant\ngetter (getHappyHour(), getMenu(), getWaiter() ...); those calls are not drawn

loop for each turn 1..18 (turns 1-5 shown)

  == Turn 1: VIP arrives, manager turns Happy Hour on, Waiter seats the VIP ==

  game -> game : **1 Arrivals** processScheduledArrival()
  game -> sched : arrival = isArrivalTurn(1)
  opt arrival
    game -> sched : vip = createArrival(1)
    create entity "vip : VIPCustomer" as vip order 80
    sched --> vip : <<create>>
    note right : status WAITING,\nsatisfaction 100
    game -> rest : registerArrival(vip)
    note right : vip joins\nwaitingQueue (FIFO)
  end
  game -> cli : displayTurnStatus(restaurant)

  game -> game : **Start of turn** processHappyHourActivation()
  game -> hh : available = isAvailable()
  opt available (once per game)
    game -> cli : choice = readHappyHourChoice()
    cli -> M : Activate Happy Hour? (y/n)
    M --> cli : y
    alt choice == y
      game -> hh : activate()
      note right : used = true, remainingTurns = 2\n(this turn and the next)
    else choice == n
      note over game, hh : Declined now: Happy Hour stays available, so it can\nstill be switched on in turn 2 (same outcome here).\nNever activated: bill $13.50, satisfaction 90
    end
  end

  game -> game : applyHappyHourRecovery()
  game -> hh : active = isActive()
  opt active
    game -> hh : amount = getRecoveryAmount()
    loop for each customer where isAwaitingService()
      game -> vip : recoverSatisfaction(amount)
      note right : 100 stays 100 (capped)
    end
  end

  game -> game : **2 Manager choices (Host)** coordinateHostAction()
  game -> cli : hostChoice = readHostChoice()
  cli -> M : Host: SEAT or WAIT?
  M --> cli : WAIT
  note right of game : The Host seats one queued customer on top of the Waiter's\ntask. WAIT here: the Waiter seats the VIP instead.

  game -> game : **2-3 Manager choices and staff actions** coordinateStaffActions()
  note over game, cli : Steps 3-4 have nothing to do until a dish is READY\nor a customer is served (drawn from turn 3)
  note over game, cli : With several candidates the manager also picks which customer\nor order goes first; here there is only one candidate
  game -> cli : choice = readWaiterChoice()
  cli -> M : Waiter: SEAT, TAKE_ORDER, SERVE or WAIT?
  M --> cli : SEAT
  opt choice == SEAT
    game -> rest : t1 = findAvailableTable()
    game -> waiter : seatCustomer(vip, t1)
    waiter -> t1 : seat(vip)
    waiter -> vip : markSeated()
    note right : WAITING to SEATED
    game -> rest : getWaitingQueue().remove(vip)
    note right : vip leaves the waitingQueue
  end
  note over game, cli : Chef: manager picks WAIT (no order exists yet)

  game -> game : **5 Waiting and abandonment** processEndOfTurnUpdates()
  game -> rest : customers = getCustomers()
  loop for each customer where isAwaitingService()
    game -> vip : reduceSatisfaction()
    vip -> vip : adjustSatisfaction(-getSatisfactionLoss())
    note right : polymorphism: loss per waiting turn\nRegular 8, VIP 5, Critic 12\n100 to 95
    game -> vip : leave = shouldAbandon()
    opt leave (satisfaction == 0)
      game -> rest : handleAbandonment(vip)
      note right : cancel order, free table, ABANDONED
    end
  end
  game -> hh : endTurn()
  note right : remainingTurns 2 to 1

  newpage Restaurant Rush: Stage 3 sequence diagram (page 2 of 3)
  == Turn 2: Happy Hour recovery, Waiter takes the order (price locked), Chef finishes it ==

  game -> game : applyHappyHourRecovery()
  game -> hh : active = isActive()
  loop for each customer where isAwaitingService()
    game -> vip : recoverSatisfaction(15)
    note right : 95 to 100 (capped at 100)
  end

  note over game, cli : Host: manager picks WAIT (no queued customer)
  game -> game : **2-3 Manager choices and staff actions** coordinateStaffActions()
  game -> cli : choice = readWaiterChoice()
  cli -> M : Waiter: SEAT, TAKE_ORDER, SERVE or WAIT?
  M --> cli : TAKE_ORDER
  opt choice == TAKE_ORDER
    game -> waiter : order = takeOrder(vip, menu, hh, 2)
    waiter -> vip : item = chooseMenuItem(menu)
    note right : documented rule: Burger\n(1500 cents, 1 unit)
    waiter -> vip : bill = calculateBillCents(1500)
    note right : polymorphism: VIPCustomer\noverrides it, 1500 x 0.9 = 1350
    waiter -> hh : bill = applyDiscount(1350)
    note right : Happy Hour active: 1350 x 0.8 = 1080\n(declined: stays 1350)
    create entity "order : Order" as order order 100
    waiter --> order : <<create>> Order(item, 1080, 2)
    note right : billCents set once in the\nconstructor, no setter:\nprice locked at 1080
    waiter -> vip : assignOrder(order)
    note right : SEATED to ORDERED
  end
  game -> cli : chefChoice = readChefChoice()
  cli -> M : Chef: choose an order or WAIT?
  M --> cli : the VIP's order
  opt chefChoice is an order
    game -> chef : prepare(order)
    chef -> order : advancePreparation()
    note right : Burger 1/1 unit: PLACED to READY.\nWaiter already acted this turn:\nserved on turn 3 at the earliest
  end

  game -> game : **5 Waiting and abandonment** processEndOfTurnUpdates()
  loop for each customer where isAwaitingService()
    game -> vip : reduceSatisfaction()
    note right : ORDERED, not yet served: 100 to 95\n(abandonment check as in turn 1)
  end
  game -> hh : endTurn()
  note right : remainingTurns 1 to 0\nHappy Hour is over

  == Turn 3: Waiter serves the dish that became READY on an earlier turn ==

  note over game, cli : Host: manager picks WAIT (no queued customer)
  game -> game : **2-3 Manager choices and staff actions** coordinateStaffActions()
  loop until the Waiter choice is accepted
    game -> cli : choice = readWaiterChoice()
    cli -> M : Waiter: SEAT, TAKE_ORDER, SERVE or WAIT?
    M --> cli : SERVE
    opt choice == SERVE
      game -> waiter : serveOrder(vip, 3)
      waiter -> order : ready = isReady()
      alt ready (finished on turn 2)
        waiter -> order : markServed()
        note right : READY to SERVED
        waiter -> vip : markServed(3)
        note right : ORDERED to SERVED, servedTurn = 3
        waiter -> order : turnTaken = getTurnTaken()
        waiter -> vip : onServed(3 - turnTaken)
        note right : polymorphic hook: Critic loses 10 if served\nmore than 2 turns after ordering;\nVIP does nothing
      else not READY (for example, SERVE tried on turn 2)
        waiter --> game : rejects: "order is not READY yet"
        game -> cli : displayError(reason)
        note right : staff task is not used up,\nthe manager chooses again
      end
    end
  end
  note over game, cli : Chef: manager picks WAIT
  note over game : **5** served customers lose no satisfaction,\nso the waiting loop has nothing to do

  newpage Restaurant Rush: Stage 3 sequence diagram (page 3 of 3)
  == Turn 4: customer 2 arrives and the Host seats them; the VIP becomes READY_TO_PAY ==

  game -> game : **1 Arrivals** processScheduledArrival()
  game -> sched : arrival = isArrivalTurn(4)
  opt arrival
    game -> sched : c2 = createArrival(4)
    game -> rest : registerArrival(c2)
    note right : customer 2 joins the waitingQueue\n(created as in turn 1; type set by the schedule)
  end
  game -> cli : displayTurnStatus(restaurant)

  game -> game : **2 Manager choices (Host)** coordinateHostAction()
  game -> cli : hostChoice = readHostChoice()
  cli -> M : Host: SEAT or WAIT?
  M --> cli : SEAT
  opt hostChoice == SEAT
    game -> rest : t2 = findAvailableTable()
    game -> host : seatCustomer(c2, t2)
    note right : Host.seatCustomer(c2, t2): markSeated() and Table.seat(c2), then\nc2 leaves the waitingQueue. The Waiter's task is not used
  end
  note over game, cli : Waiter and Chef: manager picks WAIT

  game -> game : **4 Eating and payment** coordinatePaymentProcessing()
  game -> rest : customers = getCustomers()
  loop for each customer (the vip is shown)
    game -> vip : eaten = canFinishEating(4)
    opt eaten (SERVED and 4 > servedTurn 3)
      game -> vip : markReadyToPay(4)
      note right : SERVED to READY_TO_PAY, readyTurn = 4
    end
  end
  game -> rest : payer = findOldestEligiblePayer(4)
  rest -> vip : canPay(4)
  note over game, cashier : false (4 > readyTurn 4 does not hold), so payer is null\nand the Cashier does not charge yet
  note over game, rest : **5** customer 2 is seated and unserved, so it loses its per-turn loss;\nthe VIP (READY_TO_PAY) loses nothing

  == Turn 5: Cashier collects the locked bill ==

  note over game, cli : Host: WAIT. Waiter takes customer 2's order and the Chef starts it\n(not traced further: this trace follows the VIP)
  game -> game : **4 Eating and payment** coordinatePaymentProcessing()
  note over game, rest : nobody is SERVED, so nobody new finishes eating
  game -> rest : payer = findOldestEligiblePayer(5)
  rest -> vip : canPay(5)
  note over game, cashier : true (5 > readyTurn 4): payer is the vip
  opt payer is not null
    game -> cashier : processPayment(vip, restaurant)
    cashier -> vip : order = getOrder()
    cashier -> order : bill = getBillCents()
    note right : 1080: the locked price,\nnot recalculated
    cashier -> rest : recordPayment(bill)
    note right : revenue += $10.80\nvip counts as paid with satisfaction 95
    cashier -> vip : markPaid()
    note right : READY_TO_PAY to PAID
    cashier -> order : markPaid()
    note right : SERVED to PAID
    cashier -> rest : releaseTableOf(vip)
    rest -> t1 : release()
  end

  ... turns 6 to 18 run the same six steps for the other customers ...
end

game -> game : **6 Result** evaluateGameResult()
game -> rest : paid = getPaidCustomerCount()
game -> rest : revenue = getRevenueCents()
game -> rest : avg = getAverageSatisfaction()
note right : won = paid >= 4 and revenue >= 4500\nand avg >= 60 (the game never ends early)
game -> cli : displayResult(won, restaurant)
alt won
  cli -> M : Victory
else not won
  cli -> M : Defeat
end
@enduml

##### individual/Ryan/reflection/Individual Contribution Report.pdf
/usr/bin/bash: line 1: pdfinfo: command not found
What did I learn?

I gained a better understanding of how OOP principles can be applied to accommodate
real life changing software requirements. I learned inheritance, polymorphism and
abstraction support the Open/Closed Principle by allowing new functionality to be
introduced without extensively modifying existing classes. I also improved my ability to
construct UML class diagrams and represent relationships between classes including
inheritance and associations. The project helped me see how they contribute to designing
a real system outside of only theory in lectures.

How did I apply it?

My main contribution was designing parts of Stage 2 and 3 UML class diagrams and
explaining how I implemented the SOLID principles.

For Stage 2, I focused on introducing VIPCustomer and CriticCustomer as subclasses of
the abstract Customer class. This allowed different customer types to have specialized
behaviors such as waiting satisfaction penalties, discounts and ordering preferences while
following the same general restaurant workflow.

For Stage 3, I focused on extending the menu system through ComboMeal which inherits
from MenuItem and contains two existing menu items. This design allows combo meals to
be treated as menu items withing the existing ordering structure.

These designs help me apple lecture content and understand the importance of designing
classes that can accommodate new requirements.

What challenged me?

One of the biggest challenges was determining how to incorporate the new requirements
into the existing class structure without creating unnecessary dependencies or modifying
too many classes. I initially found it difficult to decide whether new features should be
modelled as subclasses or handled by modifying existing classes.

Another challenge was constructing and presenting the UML diagrams. As more classes,
methods and relationships were introduced, the diagrams became increasingly
complicated and difficult to read. I also had limited experience using Google Colab and
Mermaid for UML diagrams, which made generating, formatting and exporting the
diagrams challenging.
How did I solve it?

To address the design challenges, I revisited the concepts of inheritance, polymorphism
and SOLID principles, particularly OCP and LSP. I examined how customer-specific
behaviors could be encapsulated within subclasses rather than implemented using
repeated conditional checks in the controller.

Similarly, for the Stage 3 menu extension, I used the existing MenuItem abstraction to
represent ComboMeal, allowing the design to reuse the ordering structure.

For the diagram-related difficulties, I experimented with different rendering and export
methods in Google Colab. After encountering issues with diagram scaling and image
generation, I exported the diagrams as PNG files instead of relying on screenshots. I also
organised the diagrams and design documentation for inclusion in the group's GitHub
repository and final report.

This experience taught me that designing extensible software requires more than applying
inheritance mechanically. It involves understanding each class's responsibility,
considering the impact of new requirements on existing components, and making design
decisions that maintain clarity and flexibility.
##### individual/Ryan/reflection/Stage 2 and 3 SOLID Principles.pdf
/usr/bin/bash: line 1: pdfinfo: command not found
Design and SOLID Principles

Stage 2
S : Single Responsibility
Where it shows: every class owns one concern

    � ArrivalSchedule -> who arrives and when
    � HappyHour -> The Happy Hour rules and timer
    � Order -> One dish's preparation progress and its locked price
    � Host/Waiter/Chef/Cashier -> one staff role each
    � RestarauntCLI -> Input and output only
    � GameController -> Turn sequencing only

Without it, GameController would hold a happyHourTurnsLeft counter, a happyHourUsed
flag, the arrival table and the discount maths.

Happy Hour's rule live entirely in HappyHour. Changing the discount from 20% to 25%
touches one constant in one class.

O: Open/Closed (Open for extension and closed for modification)
Where it shows:
Stage 2 : adding VIP and Critics meant new subclasses. The waiting-loss loop, Cashier and
Chefs were not edited.

Stage 3: adding ComboMeal meant a new subclass of MenuItem. Chef, Order and Cashier
were not edited.
Without it, adding a fourth customer type would mean finding and editing every one of
these chains: satisfaction, billing and ordering.

I : Interface Segregation ( dont force a class to depend on methods it doesnt use)
Where it shows: you deliberately did not create one big Staff superclass. Each role exposes
only its own operations:
Host has seatCustomer only.
Chef has prepare only.

abstract class Staff {
  abstract void seatCustomer(...); abstract Order takeOrder(...);
  abstract void serveOrder(...); abstract void prepare(...);
  abstract void processPayment(...);

}
Then Host has to implement prepare() and processPayment() with empty bodies or
exceptions. Those are meaningless methods, and an LSP risk too.

# Diagrams

The `.puml` files in this folder (`docs/diagrams/src/`) are the sources of truth. The `.pdf` files one level up (`docs/diagrams/`) are generated (vector); do not edit them by hand.

| Diagram | Source | PDF | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|
| Stage 1 class | `stage-1-class.puml` | `stage-1-class.pdf` | Redrawn in PlantUML from the Visual Paradigm model `stage-1-class.uml` (kept as the archive). Every class is new. |
| Stage 2 class | `stage-2-class.puml` | `stage-2-class.pdf` | Colour key: green new in Stage 2, yellow changed since Stage 1, blue unchanged. |
| Stage 3 class | `stage-3-class.puml` | `stage-3-class.pdf` | Colour key: green new in Stage 3, yellow changed since Stage 2, blue unchanged. |
| Stage 3 sequence | `stage-3-sequence.puml` | `stage-3-sequence.pdf` | A VIP orders a Burger with Happy Hour on. Turns 1 to 5 are drawn one by one over three pages (setup and turn 1; turns 2 and 3; turns 4 and 5 and the result). The Host is left at WAIT in turns 1 to 3 and seats customer 2 (the base-setting turn-4 arrival) in turn 4; customer 2 is not traced further. |

Plain accessors that no other diagram uses are left out of the class diagrams. Operations that the sequence diagram calls are always listed, and every message in the sequence diagram is an operation declared in the Stage 3 class diagram.

## Drawing conventions

- Three links meet at the top-left corner of `Customer` (`customers`, `waitingQueue` from `Restaurant`, and `occupant` from `Table`), and end labels there overlapped. Their multiplicities at the `Customer` end are written in the link label instead (`customers [0..*]`, `occupant [0..1]`). The same is done for `Customer` to `Order` (`order [0..1]`).
- `occupant` is directed from `Table`, which holds the reference. A table has at most one customer, and a customer has a table only once seated, hence `[0..1]`.
- `ComboMeal` is both a `MenuItem` (hollow-triangle line) and made of two `MenuItem` components (orange diamond line, `components [2]`). Both lines join the same two boxes, so the aggregation is coloured and its multiplicity is in the label.

## Regenerating

You need Java, a Chromium-based browser and a PlantUML jar (1.2025.4 was used):

```
node docs/diagrams/src/render.mjs path/to/plantuml.jar
```

This renders every `.puml` in this folder to a vector `.pdf` in `docs/diagrams/`. A diagram that PlantUML splits with `newpage` becomes a multi-page PDF; every page is as wide as the widest page and as tall as its own diagram. Set `CHROME` if the script cannot find your browser. If you need a PNG or SVG for a document, export it from the PDF or run PlantUML directly; those formats are not kept in the repo.

## Using the diagrams in the report

The PDFs are drawn large so that nothing overlaps. At 100% the text is 9 pt, and the page sizes are:

| PDF | Page size (pt) |
|---|---|
| `stage-1-class.pdf` | 1207 x 1460 |
| `stage-2-class.pdf` | 1481 x 1503 |
| `stage-3-class.pdf` | 1562 x 1979 |
| `stage-3-sequence.pdf` | 1792 wide; pages 1704, 1436 and 1484 tall |

Shrunk to A4 landscape width (842 pt) the text drops to about 4 pt, which is too small to read in print. Give each diagram its own full A3 landscape page (about 66%, text about 6 pt), or place it full-width on a rotated A4 page and keep the vector PDF in the repo for zooming. Import the three sequence pages as separate figures rather than as one scaled page.

The class diagrams use PlantUML's built-in Smetana layout, so Graphviz is not needed. Smetana cannot route labelled same-row links, so sideways links carry no mid-link label.

## Keeping diagrams in step with the code

Each diagram must match the code of the stage it describes. The class diagrams were aligned with the Java code at tags `stage-1`, `stage-2` and `stage-3`, and every member listed in a class diagram (name and visibility) was checked against the compiled classes of that tag. Before changing a stage, check again and re-render.

Deliberate differences between the diagrams and the code: plain accessors and private helpers are not drawn (for example `Restaurant.getTables()`, `Table.getTableId()`, `Order.getItem()`, the controller's private task methods), and the `Money` formatting helper is not drawn.

## 0066 Assistant — 2026-11-03T02:57:51.593Z

Source record: 201; UUID: 8b0d7d0f-4910-447f-b349-7f7df7d3c4f0.

**Verdict: it does not deserve full marks yet. I estimate about 78/100 as the repo stands, and 90–95 if the gaps below are closed. The code is close to full marks. The shortfall is in submission evidence, not in the game.**

I did not edit any repo files. I could not see the PDF report or the video, and headless Chrome rendered the diagram PDFs blank. So I judged the diagrams from the `.puml` sources and the text extracted from the PDFs, not from how they look on the page.

## What I checked
- **Builds and tests:** All three tags build and pass their own tests (55 / 108 / 164 checks).
- **Recorded runs:** The victory and defeat replays work at every stage and match the README (Stage 3 victory: 5 paid, $83.70, 81.2 average).
- **Invalid input:** Bad commands are rejected with a reason and do not use up the staff task.
- **Stage 1:** Contains no Stage 2 or 3 classes.
- **Rule checks:** The code meets the rubric's rules and reference prices. The code has no `instanceof` checks, and the Critic and VIP behaviour is dispatched polymorphically. `ComboMeal` is a real composition of two items, and bills are locked when the order is taken.

## Estimated marks

| Area | Max | Est. | Why |
|---|---|---|---|
| Class diagram | 25 | 22 | Complete and aligned with the code. `Menu "3..*"` contradicts the code, which needs only one item. Attributes are left out inconsistently. The Stage 3 page is 1562×1979 pt, which is hard to read at print size. |
| Sequence diagram | 20 | 17–18 | The scenario is right: a Happy Hour decision changes a VIP's bill ($10.80 vs $13.50) and satisfaction (95 vs 90). It shows READY, a later-turn serve, READY_TO_PAY, a later-turn payment, and the result check. |
| Design consideration | 15 | ~10 | Good OCP and polymorphism evidence, but I found no written alternative design or trade-offs in the repo. |
| Implementation | 20 | ~18.5 | Behaviour is correct and reproducible. Marks are lost only on code quality (below). |
| Process | 20 | ~10 | No video, tags not on GitHub, and reflections are incomplete. |

## What costs marks, most important first

1. **The tags are not on GitHub.** `git ls-remote --tags origin` returns nothing, only `main`. The README compare links will 404, and the TA's step 4 fails. Run `git push origin stage-1 stage-2 stage-3`. This is worth up to about 5 marks and takes a minute.
2. **There is no video and the README timestamps are blank.** That costs the 5 video marks. Record it and fill in the table in [README.md](README.md).
3. **The report evidence is thin.**
   - Only Ryan's reflection is in the repo. The other three members' `reflection/` folders hold only `.gitkeep`.
   - Ryan's [SOLID PDF](docs/individual/Ryan/reflection/Stage%202%20and%203%20SOLID%20Principles.pdf) contradicts the README. It credits HappyHour to Stage 2 and says Cashier and Order were untouched, but the README lists Cashier and Order as changed in Stage 2. It also has typos and is written in the second person ("you deliberately did not…").
   - No written alternative design appears anywhere. The AI chat did flag it ("HappyHour is a concrete class; discuss the design trade-off in the report"). Add one paragraph each for a Strategy-object customer design versus inheritance, `HappyHour` as a pricing-policy interface versus a concrete class, and `ComboMeal` as a subclass versus a composite, with the consequences of each.
4. **Code-quality blemishes that the rubric's "god class" and "keep rules out of the UI" lines can pick up.**
   - [GameController.java](src/restaurantrush/control/GameController.java) does turn flow, command parsing (`Command`, line 294), candidate selection (`select`, line 246) and message formatting. The staff classes are thin; `Chef.prepare` is one line. Move parsing and selection out of the controller.
   - [RestaurantCLI.java:187-218](src/restaurantrush/boundary/RestaurantCLI.java#L187-L218) recomputes which tasks are available, duplicating the controller's eligibility rules. Have the controller or domain expose "available tasks" and let the CLI only print it.
   - [Restaurant.java:98](src/restaurantrush/entity/Restaurant.java#L98) hands out the live mutable queue, and the controller removes from it directly ([GameController.java:172](src/restaurantrush/control/GameController.java#L172)). Add `Restaurant.seat(customer, table)` instead.
   - `Order.nextOrderId` is static mutable state ([Order.java:10](src/restaurantrush/entity/Order.java#L10)). The `RestaurantCLI` and `GameController` classes also reference each other.
5. **AI history is incomplete for three of four members.** This is required by the briefing but is not a rubric line.
   - Ryan's `chat-01.md` is 2 bytes (empty), and Peramudhan and Ye Fei have nothing.
   - The briefing asks for personal branches. The README says these are folders, not branches.
6. **Smaller items.**
   - `docs/diagrams/src/README.md` and `render.mjs` are gitignored, yet the `.puml` headers point to the README.
   - The 1 MB `stage-1-class.uml` is a leftover export.
   - Redraw the class diagrams so they stay readable at A3 or A4.

If you fix items 1–3, you recover roughly 12–15 marks. Item 4 is what separates 93 from 98, because it affects how a TA scores the "responsibility and cohesion" and "code quality" lines.

const tmp = fs.mkdtempSync(path.join(os.tmpdir(), 'rr-diagrams-'));
const sources = fs.readdirSync(dir).filter((f) => f.endsWith('.puml'));

for (const file of sources) {
  const name = file.replace(/\.puml$/, '');
  execFileSync('java', ['-jar', jar, '-tsvg', '-charset', 'UTF-8', '-o', tmp, path.join(dir, file)], { stdio: 'inherit' });

  // PlantUML writes name.svg, then name_001.svg, name_002.svg, ... for each newpage.
  const pageFiles = fs
    .readdirSync(tmp)
    .filter((f) => new RegExp(`^${name}(_\\d{3})?\\.svg$`).test(f))
    .sort();
  const pages = pageFiles.map((f) => {
    const svg = fs.readFileSync(path.join(tmp, f), 'utf8');
    const [, , w, h] = svg.match(/viewBox="([\d.\s]+)"/)[1].trim().split(/\s+/).map(Number);
    return { svg: svg.replace(/^<\?xml[^>]*>/, ''), width: Math.ceil(w), height: Math.ceil(h) };
  });
  const width = Math.max(...pages.map((p) => p.width));

  // One named @page per diagram page, so each PDF page gets its own height.
  const html = path.join(tmp, `${name}.html`);
  fs.writeFileSync(
    html,
    `<!doctype html><meta charset="utf-8"><style>` +
      pages.map((p, i) => `@page p${i}{size:${width}px ${p.height}px;margin:0}`).join('') +
      `html,body{margin:0;padding:0}svg{display:block}` +
      `.page{width:${width}px;overflow:hidden;break-after:page}.page:last-child{break-after:auto}</style>` +
      pages.map((p, i) => `<div class="page" style="page:p${i};height:${p.height}px">${p.svg}</div>`).join(''),
  );

  const common = ['--headless=new', '--disable-gpu', '--hide-scrollbars'];
  execFileSync(chrome, [...common, '--no-pdf-header-footer', `--print-to-pdf=${path.join(outDir, `${name}.pdf`)}`, pathToFileURL(html).href], { stdio: 'ignore' });
  console.log(`${name}: ${pages.length} page(s), ${width}px wide, heights ${pages.map((p) => p.height).join('/')}px -> ${name}.pdf`);
}
fs.rmSync(tmp, { recursive: true, force: true });

265	    private static int satisfactionAfterServiceDelay(Customer customer, int delayTurns) {

279	        int arrivals = 0;
280	        boolean vip = false;

282	        for (int turn = 1; turn <= 18; turn++) {
283	            boolean expected = turn % 3 == 1 && turn <= 16;
284	            check("arrival on turn " + turn + " is " + expected, schedule.isArrivalTurn(turn) == expected);
285	            if (schedule.isArrivalTurn(turn)) {
286	                Customer arrival = schedule.createArrival(turn);
287	                arrivals++;
288	                check("customers are numbered in arrival order", arrival.getId() == arrivals);
289	                vip |= arrival.getTypeLabel().equals("VIP");
290	                critic |= arrival.getTypeLabel().equals("Critic");
291	            }
292	        }
293	        check("six customers arrive", arrivals == 6);
294	        check("the base run includes a VIP and a Critic", vip && critic);
295	        expectRejected("no customer can be created on a non-arrival turn", () -> schedule.createArrival(2));
296	    }

{
  "file_path": "D:\\Java Projects\\SC2002GroupProject\\src\\restaurantrush\\control\\ManagerCommand.java",
  "content": "package restaurantrush.control;\n\nimport java.util.List;\nimport java.util.Locale;\n\nimport restaurantrush.entity.Customer;\n\n/**\n * One typed manager choice: a verb and an optional customer number, for example \"SERVE #3\".\n * A blank line means WAIT. It reads the text and works out which customer it refers to; whether\n * the task is allowed is decided by the staff and the domain objects.\n */\nfinal class ManagerCommand {\n    private final String verb;\n    private final Integer customerId;\n\n    private ManagerCommand(String verb, Integer customerId) {\n        this.verb = verb;\n        this.customerId = customerId;\n    }\n\n    static ManagerCommand parse(String raw) {\n        String text = raw == null ? \"\" : raw.trim().toUpperCase(Locale.ROOT);\n        if (text.isEmpty()) {\n            return new ManagerCommand(\"WAIT\", null);\n        }\n        String[] words = text.split(\"\\\\s+\");\n        if (words.length > 2) {\n            throw new IllegalArgumentException(\"Expected 'COMMAND [customer number]' but got '\" + raw.trim() + \"'\");\n        }\n        Integer id = null;\n        if (words.length == 2) {\n            String number = words[1].startsWith(\"#\") ? words[1].substring(1) : words[1];\n            try {\n                id = Integer.valueOf(number);\n            } catch (NumberFormatException notANumber) {\n                throw new IllegalArgumentException(\"'\" + words[1] + \"' is not a customer number\");\n            }\n        }\n        return new ManagerCommand(words[0], id);\n    }\n\n    String getVerb() {\n        return verb;\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Resolves the customer this command refers to among those who could take part in the task, or\n     * explains why it cannot. With no number given, a single candidate is chosen automatically.\n     */\n    Customer select(List<Customer> candidates, List<Customer> everyone, String noCandidates, String ineligible) {\n        if (customerId == null) {\n            if (candidates.isEmpty()) {\n                throw new IllegalStateException(\"Nothing to do: \" + noCandidates);\n            }\n            if (candidates.size() > 1) {\n                throw new IllegalArgumentException(\"Several customers qualify (\" + idList(candidates)\n                        + \"); add a customer number, for example \" + verb + \" \" + candidates.get(0).getId());\n            }\n            return candidates.get(0);\n        }\n        for (Customer candidate : candidates) {\n            if (candidate.getId() == customerId) {\n                return candidate;\n            }\n        }\n        for (Customer customer : everyone) {\n            if (customer.getId() == customerId) {\n                throw new IllegalStateException(customer.getDisplayName() + \" is \" + customer.getStatus()\n                        + \": \" + ineligible);\n            }\n        }\n        throw new IllegalArgumentException(\"There is no customer #\" + customerId);\n    }\n\n    private static String idList(List<Customer> customers) {\n        StringBuilder ids = new StringBuilder();\n        for (Customer customer : customers) {\n            ids.append(ids.length() == 0 ? \"#\" : \", #\").append(customer.getId());\n        }\n        return ids.toString();\n    }\n}\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "D:\\Java Projects\\SC2002GroupProject\\src\\restaurantrush\\control\\StaffActions.java",
  "content": "package restaurantrush.control;\n\nimport java.util.Optional;\nimport java.util.function.BiConsumer;\n\nimport restaurantrush.entity.Customer;\nimport restaurantrush.entity.CustomerStatus;\nimport restaurantrush.entity.Money;\nimport restaurantrush.entity.MenuItem;\nimport restaurantrush.entity.Order;\nimport restaurantrush.entity.Restaurant;\nimport restaurantrush.entity.Table;\n\n/**\n * Carries out a manager's command with the Host, the Waiter or the Chef. Each method picks the\n * customer the command refers to, asks the staff member to do the task and returns the sentence\n * for the screen (empty for WAIT). An impossible command throws before anything is changed, so\n * the controller can ask again without the staff member having used the task.\n */\nfinal class StaffActions {\n    private final Restaurant restaurant;\n\n    StaffActions(Restaurant restaurant) {\n        this.restaurant = restaurant;\n    }\n\n    Optional<String> host(ManagerCommand command) {\n        return switch (command.getVerb()) {\n            case \"WAIT\" -> Optional.empty();\n            case \"SEAT\" -> Optional.of(seat(command, restaurant.getHost()::seatCustomer, \"Host\"));\n            default -> throw new IllegalArgumentException(\n                    \"Unknown Host command '\" + command.getVerb() + \"'. Use SEAT or WAIT.\");\n        };\n    }\n\n    Optional<String> waiter(ManagerCommand command, int currentTurn) {\n        return switch (command.getVerb()) {\n            case \"WAIT\" -> Optional.empty();\n            case \"SEAT\" -> Optional.of(seat(command, restaurant.getWaiter()::seatCustomer, \"Waiter\"));\n            case \"TAKE_ORDER\", \"ORDER\", \"TAKE\" -> Optional.of(takeOrder(command, currentTurn));\n            case \"SERVE\" -> Optional.of(serve(command, currentTurn));\n            default -> throw new IllegalArgumentException(\n                    \"Unknown Waiter command '\" + command.getVerb() + \"'. Use SEAT, TAKE_ORDER, SERVE or WAIT.\");\n        };\n    }\n\n    Optional<String> chef(ManagerCommand command) {\n        return switch (command.getVerb()) {\n            case \"WAIT\" -> Optional.empty();\n            case \"COOK\", \"PREPARE\", \"ADVANCE\" -> Optional.of(cook(command));\n            default -> throw new IllegalArgumentException(\n                    \"Unknown Chef command '\" + command.getVerb() + \"'. Use COOK or WAIT.\");\n        };\n    }\n\n    private String seat(ManagerCommand command, BiConsumer<Customer, Table> seater, String actor) {\n        Customer customer = command.select(restaurant.getWaitingQueue(), restaurant.getCustomers(),\n                \"nobody is in the waiting queue\", \"only a customer in the waiting queue can be seated\");\n        Table table = restaurant.findAvailableTable();\n        if (table == null) {\n            throw new IllegalStateException(\"Both tables are occupied; a table frees up when its customer pays or leaves\");\n        }\n        restaurant.seatFromQueue(customer, table, seater);\n        return actor + \" seats \" + customer.getDisplayName() + \" at Table \" + table.getTableId() + \".\";\n    }\n\n    private String takeOrder(ManagerCommand command, int currentTurn) {\n        Customer customer = command.select(restaurant.getCustomersWithStatus(CustomerStatus.SEATED),\n                restaurant.getCustomers(), \"no seated customer is waiting to order\", \"only a seated customer can order\");\n        Order order = restaurant.getWaiter().takeOrder(customer, restaurant.getMenu(), restaurant.getHappyHour(), currentTurn);\n        MenuItem item = order.getItem();\n        return \"Waiter takes the order of \" + customer.getDisplayName() + \". They choose \"\n                + item.getName() + \" (list price \" + Money.format(item.getPriceCents()) + \", \"\n                + item.getPreparationUnits() + \" preparation unit(s)). Bill locked at \"\n                + Money.format(order.getBillCents()) + \".\";\n    }\n\n    private String serve(ManagerCommand command, int currentTurn) {\n        Customer customer = command.select(restaurant.getCustomersWithStatus(CustomerStatus.ORDERED),\n                restaurant.getCustomers(), \"no customer has an order to serve\",\n                \"only a customer who has ordered can be served\");\n        restaurant.getWaiter().serveOrder(customer, currentTurn);\n        return \"Waiter serves the \" + customer.getOrder().getItem().getName() + \" to \"\n                + customer.getDisplayName() + \".\";\n    }\n\n    private String cook(ManagerCommand command) {\n        Customer customer = command.select(restaurant.getCustomersWithStatus(CustomerStatus.ORDERED),\n                restaurant.getCustomers(), \"no order has been taken yet\",\n                \"only a customer who has ordered has food to prepare\");\n        Order order = customer.getOrder();\n        restaurant.getChef().prepare(order);\n        return \"Chef works on the \" + order.getItem().getName() + \" for \" + customer.getDisplayName()\n                + \": \" + order.getCompletedUnits() + \"/\" + order.getItem().getPreparationUnits()\n                + \" units, order is \" + order.getStatus() + \".\";\n    }\n}\n"
}

GameController {\n    private static final int MAX_TURNS = 18;\n\n    private final Restaurant restaurant;\n    private final ArrivalSchedule schedule;\n    private final StaffActions staff;\n    private int currentTurn;\n    private final int maxTurns;\n    private final RestaurantCLI cli;\n\n    public GameController(RestaurantCLI cli) {\n        this.cli = cli;\n        this.restaurant = early ending), then reports victory or defeat. */\n    public void startGame() {\n        cli.displayIntro(maxTurns);\n        while (currentTurn < maxTurns) {\n            currentTurn++;\n and 16. */\n    private void processScheduledArrival() {\n        if (schedule.isArrivalTurn(currentTurn)) {\n            restaurant.registerArrival(schedule.createArrival(currentTurn));\n        }\n    }\n\n    /** Start of turn: while Happy Hour is unused the manager may switch it on (once per game). */\n    private void processHappyHourActivation() {\n        HappyHour happyHour = restaurant.getHappyHour();\n        if (happyHour.isAvailable() && cli.readHappyHourChoice() && happyHour.activate()) {\n            cli.displayMessage(\"Happy Hour is ON: orders taken now get a discount and waiting customers recover \"\n                    + \"satisfaction. Turns left: \" + happyHour.getRemainingTurns() + \".\");\n        }\n    }\n\n    /** Start of an active Happy Hour turn: customers still waiting for a table or food recover satisfaction. */\n    private void applyHappyHourRecovery() {\n        HappyHour happyHour = restaurant.getHappyHour();\n        if (!happyHour.isActive()) {\n            return;\n        }\n        int amount = happyHour.getRecoveryAmount();\n        List<String> changes = new ArrayList<>();\n        for (Customer customer : restaurant.getCustomers()) {\n            if (customer.isAwaitingService()) {\n                int before = customer.getSatisfaction();\n                customer.recoverSatisfaction(amount);\n                changes.add(\"#\" + customer.getId() + \" \" + before + \" -> \" + customer.getSatisfaction());\n            }\n        }\n        cli.displayMessage(\"Happy Hour (\" + happyHour.getRemainingTurns() + \" turn(s) left including this one): \"\n                + (changes.isEmpty() ? \"nobody is waiting.\" : \"waiting customers recover \" + String.join(\", \", changes) + \".\"));\n    }\n\n    /** Step 2 (Host): at most one queued customer is seated, on top of the Waiter's own task. */\n    private void coordinateHostAction() {\n        chooseUntilAccepted(cli::readHostChoice, staff::host);\n    }\n\n    /** Steps 2 and 3: the Waiter acts first, then the Chef. A rejected choice does not use up the task. */\n    private void coordinateStaffActions() {\n        chooseUntilAccepted(cli::readWaiterChoice, command -> staff.waiter(command, currentTurn));\n        chooseUntilAccepted(cli::readChefChoice, staff::chef);\n    }\n\n    private void chooseUntilAccepted(Supplier<String> reader, Function<ManagerCommand, Optional<String>> task) {\n        while (true) {\n            String choice = reader.get();\n            try {\n                task.apply(ManagerCommand.parse(choice)).ifPresent(cli::displayMessage);\n                return;\n            } catch (IllegalArgumentException | IllegalStateException rejected) {\n                cli.displayError(rejected.getMessage());\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    /** Step 4: served customers finish eating, then the Cashier charges at most one READY_TO_PAY customer. */\n    private void coordinatePaymentProcessing() {\n        for (Customer customer : restaurant.getCustomers()) {\n            if (customer.canFinishEating(currentTurn)) {\n                customer.markReadyToPay(currentTurn);\n                cli.displayMessage(customer.getDisplayName() + \" finishes eating and is ready to pay.\");\n            }\n        }\n        Customer payer = restaurant.findOldestEligiblePayer(currentTurn);\n        if (payer != null) {\n            int bill = payer.getOrder().getBillCents();\n            restaurant.getCashier().processPayment(payer, restaurant);\n            cli.displayMessage(\"Cashier collects \" + restaurant.formatMoney(bill) + \" from \" + payer.getDisplayName()\n                    + \" (satisfaction \" + payer.getSatisfaction() + \"). The table is free again.\");\n        }\n    }\n\n    /** Step 5: customers who are still waiting lose satisfaction; at 0 they leave without paying. */\n    private void processEndOfTurnUpdates() {\n        for (Customer customer : restaurant.getCustomers()) {\n            if (!customer.isAwaitingService()) {\n                continue;\n            }\n            customer.reduceSatisfaction();\n            if (customer.shouldAbandon()) {\n                restaurant.handleAbandonment(customer);\n                cli.displayMessage(customer.getDisplayName()\n                        + \" runs out of patience and leaves without paying. Any unfinished order is cancelled.\");\n            }\n        }\n        restaurant.getHappyHour().endTurn();\n    }\n}\n"
}

  "content": "package restaurantrush;\n\nimport java.io.FileInputStream;\nimport java.io.IOException;\nimport java.io.InputStream;\nimport java.io.PrintStream;\nimport java.util.NoSuchElementException;\n\nimport restaurantrush.boundary.RestaurantCLI;\nimport restaurantrush.control.GameController;\n\n/**\n * Entry point, and the one place that wires the command-line boundary to the controller. With no\n * arguments the manager types the choices. With \"--input choices.txt\" the choices are read from a\n * file instead (and echoed), which replays a recorded run.\n */\npublic final class Main {\n    private Main() {\n    }\n\n    public static void main(String[] args) {\n        if (args.length == 0) {\n            play(System.in, System.out, false);\n        } else if (args.length == 2 && args[0].equals(\"--input\")) {\n            try (InputStream choices = new FileInputStream(args[1])) {\n                play(choices, System.out, true);\n            } catch (IOException unreadable) {\n                System.err.println(\"Cannot read the choices file '\" + args[1] + \"': \" + unreadable.getMessage());\n                System.exit(1);\n            }\n        } else {\n            System.err.println(\"Usage: java -cp out restaurantrush.Main [--input choices.txt]\");\n            System.exit(2);\n        }\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Plays one game reading the manager's choices from the given stream. If the input ends before\n     * the game does, the run stops with a message. echoInput repeats each line read, so a run fed\n     * from a file reads like a transcript.\n     */\n    public static void play(InputStream in, PrintStream out, boolean echoInput) {\n        RestaurantCLI cli = new RestaurantCLI(in, out, echoInput);\n        try {\n            new GameController(cli).startGame();\n        } catch (NoSuchElementException inputEnded) {\n            cli.displayInputEnded();\n        }\n    }\n}\n"
}

The Waiter's task is not used\n  end`, `  game -> cli : displayTurnStatus(restaurant, 4, 18)\n\n  game -> game : **2 Manager choices (Host)** coordinateHostAction()\n  game -> cli : hostChoice = readHostChoice()\n  cli -> M : Host: SEAT or WAIT?\n  M --> cli : SEAT\n  game -> cmd : cmd = parse(hostChoice)\n  game -> staff : message = host(cmd)\n  opt cmd.getVerb() == SEAT\n    staff -> rest : c2 = the only queued customer (select), t2 = findAvailableTable()\n    staff -> rest : seatFromQueue(c2, t2, host::seatCustomer)\n    rest -> host : seatCustomer(c2, t2)\n    note right : Host.seatCustomer(c2, t2): markSeated() and Table.seat(c2), then Restaurant\\\\nremoves c2 from the waitingQueue. The Waiter's task is not used\n  end\n  staff --> game : \"Host seats Customer #2 (Regular) at Table 2.\"\n  game -> cli : displayMessage(message)`);\n\n// ---- result ----\nrep(`game -> game : **6 Result** evaluateGameResult()\ngame -> rest : paid = getPaidCustomerCount()\ngame -> rest : revenue = getRevenueCents()\ngame -> rest : avg = getAverageSatisfaction()\nnote right : won = paid >= 4 and revenue >= 4500\\\\nand avg >= 60 (the game never ends early)\ngame -> cli : displayResult(won, restaurant)`, `game -> rest : **6 Result** won = hasMetObjective()\nnote right : paid customers >= 4 and revenue >= 4500\\\\nand average satisfaction >= 60, from its own books\\\\n(the game never ends early)\ngame -> cli : displayResult(won, restaurant, 18)`);\n\nfs.writeFileSync(p, s);\nconsole.log(\"ok\");\n"

  "content": "// Checks every class member (name + visibility) in a PlantUML class diagram against javap output.\nconst fs = require(\"fs\");\nconst cp = require(\"child_process\");\nconst [pumlPath, classesDir] = process.argv.slice(2);\nconst puml = fs.readFileSync(pumlPath, \"utf8\");\nconst pkgs = [\"entity\", \"control\", \"boundary\", \"\"];\nlet problems = 0;\nlet checked = 0;\n\nconst blocks = [...puml.matchAll(/^(?:abstract class|class|enum)\\s+(\\w+)[^{\\n]*\\{\\n([\\s\\S]*?)^\\}/gm)];\nfor (const [, name, body] of blocks) {\n  let found = null;\n  for (const pkg of pkgs) {\n    const fq = [\"restaurantrush\", pkg, name].filter(Boolean).join(\".\");\n    try {\n      found = cp.execFileSync(\"javap\", [\"-p\", \"-cp\", classesDir, fq], { encoding: \"utf8\", stdio: [\"ignore\", \"pipe\", \"ignore\"] });\n      break;\n    } catch (e) { /* try next package */ }\n  }\n  if (!found) { console.log(`NO CLASS: ${name}`); problems++; continue; }\n  const members = found.split(\"\\n\").slice(2).map((l) => l.trim()).filter((l) => l && l !== \"}\");\n  const actual = new Map(); // name -> set of visibility chars\n  for (const line of members) {\n    const vis = /\\bpublic\\b/.test(line) ? \"+\" : /\\bprivate\\b/.test(line) ? \"-\" : /\\bprotected\\b/.test(line) ? \"#\" : \"~\";\n    const isMethod = line.includes(\"(\");\n    const m = isMethod ? line.match(/(\\w+)\\(/) : line.match(/(\\w+);$/);\n    if (!m) continue;\n    const key = (isMethod ? \"m:\" : \"f:\") + m[1];\n    if (!actual.has(key)) actual.set(key, new Set());\n    actual.get(key).add(vis);\n  }\n  const ctor = name;\n  for (let raw of body.split(\"\\n\")) {\n    raw = raw.trim().replace(/\\{(static|abstract)\\}\\s*/g, \"\");\n    const m = raw.match(/^([+\\-#~])\\s*(?:\\{\\w+\\}\\s*)?(\\w+)\\s*(\\(|:)/);\n    if (!m) continue;\n    const [, vis, member, kind] = m;\n    const isMethod = kind === \"(\";\n    const key = (isMethod ? \"m:\" : \"f:\") + member;\n    checked++;\n    const vs = actual.get(key);\n    if (!vs || !vs.has(vis)) {\n      console.log(`MISMATCH ${name}.${member} drawn '${vis}' actual ${vs ? [...vs].join(\"\") : \"missing\"}`);\n      problems++;\n    }\n  }\n  // enum constants are not members drawn with visibility; ignore\n}\nconsole.log(`${checked} members checked, ${problems} problem(s)`);\n"
}

They are followed by the diagram and documentation commits and by one clean-up commit, `74c50eb`, with its diagram commit `ba84062` (see \"Stage 3 clean-up\" below).\n\n**New files**\n\n| File | Responsibility |\n|---|---|\n| `entity/ComboMeal.java` | A menu item made of two existing menu items (composition); computes the 90% price and the summed preparation units |\n| `entity/HappyHour.java` | The pricing policy: once per game, two turns, 20% off new orders, +15 satisfaction for waiting customers |\n| `entity/Host.java` | The new staff role: seats one queued customer per turn on top of the Waiter's task |\n| `control/ManagerCommand.java` | Clean-up: reads one typed manager choice (a verb and an optional customer number) and picks the customer it refers to |\n| `control/StaffActions.java` | Clean-up: carries out a Host, Waiter or Chef command and returns the sentence to show; an impossible command throws before anything changes |\n\n**Existing files changed, and why**\n\n| File | Change | Reason |\n|---|---|---|\n| `entity/MenuItem.java` | No longer `final` (one word) | `ComboMeal` extends it, so Waiter, Chef, Cashier, Menu and Order treat a combo like any dish and need no change |\n| `entity/Customer.java` | Added `recoverSatisfaction`; `isAwaitingService()` became public; clean-up: added `getDisplayName()` | Happy Hour recovery and the controller select customers who are still waiting. The existing satisfaction clamp (`adjustSatisfaction`) is reused. `getDisplayName()` gives every message one way to name a customer |\n| `entity/Waiter.java` | `takeOrder` also takes the `HappyHour` and applies it after the customer's own price | Happy Hour is applied after the VIP discount and locked in the Order. Seating, serving and the Critic hook are untouched |\n| `entity/Restaurant.java` | Owns a `Host` and a `HappyHour` (with getters); the menu now lists two combos. Clean-up: `getWaitingQueue()` is read-only and `seatFromQueue` is the only way out of the queue; `getCustomersWithStatus`, `getSeatableCustomers`, `getCustomersToServe` and `getCustomersToCook` say what each staff member can do now; the three victory targets and `hasMetObjective()` | Registration of the new classes and of the combos, once, at set-up. The clean-up gives the queue and the staff-task rules one owner: the controller used to remove customers from the live queue itself, and the screen and the controller each worked out what a staff member can do. Payment and abandonment logic is untouched |\n| `entity/Order.java` | Clean-up: removed the unused `orderId` and its static counter | Nothing read the id, and a static counter is global state shared by every game. Status rules and the locked bill are untouched |\n| `control/GameController.java` | New steps before the staff choices: Happy Hour activation and recovery, and the Host choice; `HappyHour.endTurn()` at the end of the turn; the waiting loop uses `isAwaitingService()`. Clean-up: command parsing and task execution moved to `ManagerCommand` and `StaffActions`; the result check moved to `Restaurant.hasMetObjective()` | The Stage 3 turn needs the new steps. The clean-up leaves the controller sequencing the six steps (324 to 150 lines). The Waiter and Chef logic, payment and result rule are unchanged |\n| `boundary/RestaurantCLI.java` | Host and Happy Hour prompts, Happy Hour status line, Host task line. Clean-up: no longer references `GameController`; the turn number and targets are passed in; the task lines print what `Restaurant` reports | Display and input only. Boundary and control no longer depend on each other |\n| `Main.java` | Clean-up: creates the CLI and the controller (`Main.play`) and handles the end of the input | So that `RestaurantCLI` does not need the controller |\n| `runs/victory.txt`, `runs/defeat.txt` | Replaced | There are two more prompts (Happy Hour and Host), and the menu changes what customers order. The Stage 2 runs are in the `stage-2` tag |\n| `test/restaurantrush/GameTests.java` | Extended | See below |\n\n**Unchanged:** `Menu`, `Table`, `Chef`, `Cashier`, `ArrivalSchedule`, `RegularCustomer`, `VIPCustomer`, `CriticCustomer`, the enums, `Money`.\n\n**Deleted:** nothing.\n\n**Stage 3 clean-up (no behaviour change).** After the Stage 3 features worked, review found structure the features did not need but the design should not keep: a controller that also parsed commands and picked customers, the screen repeating the staff-task rules, a live queue handed out for the controller to edit, a static order counter, and a controller and screen that referenced each other. The clean-up commit `74c50eb` fixes these in the files listed above and touches no rule: the Waiter, Host, Chef, Cashier, `HappyHour`, `ComboMeal` and the customer types are unchanged. Evidence that behaviour is the same: `runs/victory.txt`, `runs/defeat.txt` and an invalid-input session print byte-identical transcripts before and after, and every earlier check still passes.\n\n**What still works.** `GameTests` keeps every Stage 1 and Stage 2 check (the Stage 1 checks that used the three-dish menu now build that menu explicitly, because the game menu has five entries) and adds the combo reference values, Happy Hour timing, discount, price lock and recovery, the Host, and (clean-up) the queue protection, staff task lists and objective check. The recorded runs replay the whole game. See [docs/test-notes.md](docs/test-notes.md)."

Issues 1 and 4 are done and pushed. The three stage tags are now on GitHub, and the compare links resolve. I checked them through the authenticated `gh`, which shows stage-1…stage-2 as 6 commits and 19 files, and stage-2…stage-3 as 12 commits and 23 files.

## Issue 1: tags
- I pushed `main` and `stage-1`, `stage-2` and `stage-3`.
- I moved the local `stage-3` tag from `aec304a` to the final commit `f8e111c` first. It had never been pushed, so nobody saw the old position. The tag now matches the cleaned-up code and the updated diagrams.
- **The repo is private.** Plain `curl` gets 404 on the compare links, and the collaborator list is only the four of you. A TA without access will hit the same wall, so make it public or add the TA before you submit.

## Issue 4: code quality
The behaviour is unchanged. Both recorded runs and my invalid-input session print byte-identical transcripts before and after. A clean export of the final commit builds with the README command and passes 189 checks (164 before).

| Blemish | Fix |
|---|---|
| Controller did everything | [GameController](src/restaurantrush/control/GameController.java) now only sequences the six steps, going from 324 to 150 lines. Parsing moved to the new [ManagerCommand](src/restaurantrush/control/ManagerCommand.java) and task execution to the new [StaffActions](src/restaurantrush/control/StaffActions.java). |
| Screen repeated staff-task rules | [Restaurant](src/restaurantrush/entity/Restaurant.java) now reports who can be seated, cooked for or served. [RestaurantCLI](src/restaurantrush/boundary/RestaurantCLI.java) only prints it. |
| Live queue handed out | The queue is read-only, and a customer leaves it only through `seatFromQueue`, after the Waiter or Host accepted the seating. |
| Static order id | `Order.orderId` and its static counter are removed, since nothing read the id. |
| CLI ↔ controller cycle | The CLI no longer references the controller. `Main` creates both, and the victory targets moved to `Restaurant`. |

I also updated the class and sequence diagrams and regenerated their PDFs, and I checked all 180 class-diagram members against the compiled classes with `javap`, with no mismatches. The README's stage 2→3 tables and `docs/test-notes.md` now list the new and changed files with reasons.

## Trade-offs
- **Bigger Stage 3 diff.** The clean-up is part of Stage 3, so the stage 2→3 diff is larger. The README has a "Stage 3 clean-up (no behaviour change)" paragraph that justifies it.
- **Wider diagrams.** The class diagram is now 2534×3231 (was 2083×2639) and the sequence pages are 2829 wide (was 1792). The sizes in your local, gitignored `docs/diagrams/src/README.md` are now stale.
- **Tooling I added.** I downloaded the official PlantUML 1.2025.4 jar, the version your README names, into my scratch folder to regenerate the PDFs. It is not in the repo.

Issues 2, 3, 5 and 6 are untouched. The video, the other members' reflections and AI histories, and the written alternative-design paragraphs are still open.
